BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area of
Operation of Water User Association (WUAs) on 54 SÉEò xÉÆ./BxÉ 54 11 86
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act,
2005, I, Prashant A. Gode, Executive Engineer, Ambhora 56 SÉEò xÉÆ./BxÉ 56 11 65
Lift Irrigation Division, Bhiwapur hereby, delineate Area of
Operation of following WUAs and direct that the
58 SÉEò xÉÆ./BxÉ 58 11 15 certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders
and/or occupiers of said WUAs shall be displayed on the
notice board of the offices of concerned Gram Panchayat,
60 SÉEò xÉÆ./BxÉ 60 7 13 Tahsil Offices, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division and
Division and at other prominent public places.
62 SÉEò xÉÆ./BxÉ 62 11 20 Now, therefore, I, Prashant A. Gode, Executive
Engineer, Ambhora Lift Irrigation Division, Bhiwapur also
hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by the
64 SÉEò xÉÆ./BxÉ 64 9 00 Appropriate Authority to an individual holder or occupier of
such land and that system of supply of water through
Water Users Association shall be binding on all the hold-
------------ ers and occupiers of the land under Management of Irri-
-------------
Any person affected by this notification or part
thereof, may, within thirty days from the date of

BY EXECUTIVE ENGINEER
MMISF ACT, 2005.
-----------
No. 598-IB-Noti-3-Bagh Project -2024.—
-----------
Whereas, it has been decided to delineate Area of
hydraulic basis and as per Administrative convenience
under Sections 5, 6, 7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act,
2005, I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive Engineer, Bagh
Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia hereby, delineate Area
and/or occupiers of said WUAs shall be displayed on the
notice board of the offices of concerned Gram Panchayat,
Now, therefore, I, Rajiv Gosai Kurekar, Executive
------------ Engineer, Bagh Itiyadoh Irrigation Division, Gondia also
hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by the
Appropriate Authority to an individual holder or occupier of
such land and that system of supply of water through
-------------
Water User Association shall be binding on all the hold-
Irrigation System by Farmers.
-------------
Any person affected by this notification or part
thereof, may, within thirty days from the date of

publication of this notification in the Official Gazette, file

an appeal before Superintending Engineer, Bhandara,
